# macOS — inferred

The **7,221** projects that probably run on macOS but do not say so. Each verdict here comes from the install route or the language runtime, never from a release asset or from the README — that is what puts them on this page rather than with the [2,029 confirmed ones](macos.md).

Read `Why` before trusting a row. A portable runtime is a good bet and not a promise.

[← macOS](macos.md) · [← back to the mega list](../README.md)

Page **4** of 10, because this list is longer than the 512 KB GitHub will render in one file. In order: [1](macos-inferred.md) · [2](macos-inferred-2.md) · [3](macos-inferred-3.md) · **4** · [5](macos-inferred-5.md) · [6](macos-inferred-6.md) · [7](macos-inferred-7.md) · [8](macos-inferred-8.md) · [9](macos-inferred-9.md) · [10](macos-inferred-10.md) — [continue on page 5 →](macos-inferred-5.md)
